## `AUS-2652-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AUS` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Barometric-Leak Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Watch Movement Timegrapher Daily Rate & Hydrostatic Depth
- **Intro:** Barometric-Leak Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Watch Movement Timegrapher Daily Rate & Hydrostatic Depth — Pre-Operational Verification and Environmental Stabilization: Temperature: 23±1°C; Humidity: 45±5% RH; Insulation resistance: ≥1.0 MΩ; Calibration certificate validity: ≤90 days.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 23±1°C; Humidity: 45±5% RH; Insulation resistance: ≥1.0 MΩ; Calibration certificate validity: ≤90 days.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9001:2016`, `AS/NZS 3760:2010`, `ISO 3158:1976`, `ISO 6425:2018`
